<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/** Article body. Long-form text uses `typeset typeset-docs`; components inside carry `not-typeset`. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> Article</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">() {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> className</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"typeset typeset-docs max-w-reading"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">        Tessel runs every session in a sandbox, but some actions reach outside it: pushing a branch, opening a pull request, calling a</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">        paid API. Approval policies decide which of those run on their own, which wait for a person and which are never allowed.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">        Policies live in &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">code</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>tessel.toml&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">code</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> next to your code, so they are reviewed like any other change. Workspace admins can</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">        add an org-wide floor that repositories can tighten but never loosen.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">h2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> id</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"how-it-works"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>How approvals work&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">h2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">        Before a tool call leaves the sandbox, Tessel matches it against your rules from top to bottom. The first rule that matches</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">        wins. When nothing matches, the &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">code</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>default&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">code</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> applies.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">ol</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">        &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">li</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>The agent proposes a call with its exact arguments.&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">li</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">        &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">li</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>Tessel looks up the decision for that tool and those arguments.&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">li</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">        &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">li</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">          An &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">strong</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>ask&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">strong</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> decision pauses the session and shows an approval card in the workspace, Slack and the PR thread.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">        &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">li</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">        &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">Alert</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> variant</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"info"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">            &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">AlertDescription</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>A pending approval only blocks the step that needs it.
